k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}X_{j}X_{i}\Big\|_{p}.

We will bound each terms on the right hand side successively. Firstly, we note that

\|X_{i}^{2}-\sigma^{2}\|_{p}\leq(R^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2})^{1-1/p}\penalty\ \textrm{for $p\geq 2$.}

Hence according to
lemma

K.2
, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{1}{2n}\Big((n-k)\sum_{1\leq i\leq k}(X_{i}^{2}-\sigma^{2})+k\sum_{k<i\leq n}(X_{i}^{2}-\sigma^{2})\Big)\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}{2\sqrt{n}}\min\begin{cases}\sqrt{p-1}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2})^{1-1/p}\\
A_{p}\sigma\sqrt{R^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2}}+A^{*}_{n,p}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}_{s}-\sigma^{2})^{1-1/p}.\end{cases}

where recall that
A_{p}:=2^{1/p}\sqrt{p/2+1}e^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{p}}
and
A_{n,p}^{*}:=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{p}{2}+1}}\right)n^{1/p-1/2}
.

Now we need to upper bound
\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}X_{j}X_{i}\|_{p}
. We remark that as
(X_{1},\dots,X_{k})
and
(X_{k+1},\dots,X_{n})
are independent, using according to Lemma 11 and Lemma 14 in
[
2
]
, the following holds

\displaystyle\sigma^{-2}\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}X_{j}X_{i}\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle=\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq k\end{subarray}}X_{i}/\sigma\Big\|_{p}\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k+1\leq i\leq n\end{subarray}}X_{i}/\sigma\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\{\sqrt{k}\min\begin{cases}\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}\\
A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{k,p}\end{cases}\Big\}\times\Big\{\sqrt{n-k}\min\begin{cases}\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}\\
A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{n-k,p}\end{cases}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{k(n-k)}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p},A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{k,p}\Big\}^{2}.

Therefore, if we combine everything together we obtain that

\displaystyle\sigma^{-2}\Big\|\frac{1}{2s}\mathbb{E}\Big[(W^{\prime}_{k}-W_{k})^{2}\Big|W_{k}\Big]-\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}{\sqrt{n}}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p},A_{p}\sqrt{\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1}+A^{*}_{n,p}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p}\Big\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p},A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{k,p}\Big\}^{2}\Big).

This immediately implies the desired result by Jensen’s inequality.
∎

Lemma C.9
.

The following equality holds for all
p\geq 2
:

\displaystyle(a_{2})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{n}\|H_{1}\|_{p}}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p},A_{p}\sqrt{\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1}+A^{*}_{n,p}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p}\Big\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p},A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{k,p}\Big\}^{2}\Big]\Big(1-\frac{R}{\sqrt{\kappa}\sigma_{n,k}}\Big).

Proof.

Recall that

(a_{2})=\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-2t}\|H_{1}\|_{p}}{\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}}}\Big\|\frac{1}{2s}\sigma_{n,k}^{-2}\mathbb{E}\big[(W^{\prime}_{k}-W_{k})^{2}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})\big]-1\Big\|_{p}dt.

By
lemma

C.8
, we have

\displaystyle(a_{2})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p},A_{p}\sqrt{\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1}+A^{*}_{n,p}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p}\Big\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p},A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{k,p}\Big\}^{2}\Big]\|H_{1}\|_{p}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-2t}}{\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}}}dt

\displaystyle\overset{}{\leq}

\displaystyle\|H_{1}\|_{p}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p},A_{p}\sqrt{\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1}+A^{*}_{n,p}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p}\Big\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p},A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{k,p}\Big\}^{2}\Big]\Big(1-\frac{R}{\sqrt{\kappa}\sigma_{n,k}}\Big).

∎

C.2.5
Bounding
(a_{3})

Lemma C.10
.

For all odd integer
\ell\geq 3
, the following inequality holds for
p>2
:

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\sqrt{p-1}\sqrt{k(n-k)}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}\bigg((n-k)(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}

\displaystyle\qquad\penalty\ +k\min\Big\{(2^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\frac{2}{p}}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}})^{2},\Big((R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}\penalty\ +\frac{R^{\frac{4}{p}}}{\sqrt{k}\sigma^{2/p}}\min\{\sqrt{p-1},A_{p}+A^{*}_{k,p}\}\Big)^{2}\Big\}\bigg)^{1/2}.

Moreover, for
p\geq 4
, the following inequality also holds:

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}C_{p}\bigg\{R^{\ell-2}\sqrt{(n-k)k}\sqrt{(n-k)\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})+k{(R^{2}\sigma^{2}+R^{4-\frac{4}{p}})}}

\displaystyle+k^{\frac{1}{p}}(n-k)^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\bigg(k^{p-1}\Big(\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{1/p}+2^{1/p}k^{-\frac{1}{2}}R^{2/p}\Big)^{p}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(n-k)^{p-1}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})\bigg)^{\frac{1}{p}}\bigg\},

where
C_{p}:=2\sqrt{2}\Big(\frac{p}{4}+1\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}(1+\frac{p}{\log(p/2)})
.

Proof.

First, note that by the definition of
W_{k}^{\prime}
, the difference
W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime}
is equal to
X_{I}-X_{J}
. Since
I\sim\mathrm{Unif}\{1,\dots,k\}
and
J\sim\mathrm{Unif}\{k+1,\dots,n\}
, we have
\mathbb{P}(I=i,J=j)=\frac{1}{k(n-k)}
for any
i\leq k
and
k<j\leq n
. Consequently, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})]\Big\|_{p}\leq

\displaystyle s^{-1}\frac{1}{k(n-k)}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(X_{i}-X_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(X_{i}-X_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}.

To bound the right hand side, we define the following filtration

\mathcal{F}_{m}:=\sigma(Y_{1},\dots,Y_{m}),

and define
(D_{m})_{m}
to be the following martingale difference

D_{m}=\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{m}\Big]-\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{m-1}\Big].

We remark that, as
\ell
is odd, it follows that
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}\big[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\big]=0
. Hence by a telescopic sum argument, we notice that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}=\sum_{m\leq n}D_{m}.

Now using the Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund inequality (
lemma

K.3
), we obtain that

\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}\leq\sqrt{p-1}\sqrt{\sum_{m=1}^{n}\|D_{m}\|_{p}^{2}}.

Moreover according to Theorem 1 of
[
40
]
, for
p\geq 4
we have:

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq C_{p}\Big(\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\sum_{m\leq n}\mathbb{E}[D_{m}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{m-1}]\Big)^{\frac{p}{2}}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}+\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{m\leq n}D_{m}^{p}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}\Big)

(20)

\displaystyle\leq C_{p}\Big(\sqrt{\sum_{m\leq n}\Big\|\mathbb{E}[D_{m}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{m-1}]\Big\|_{p/2}}+\Big(\sum_{m\leq n}\|D_{m}\|_{p}^{p}\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}\Big).

(21)

where
C_{p}:=2\sqrt{2}\Big(\frac{p}{4}+1\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}(1+\frac{p}{\log(p/2)})
.

Each one of those inequalities will be further bounded. This is done by getting a bound on both
\|D_{m}\|_{p}
and
\mathbb{E}[D_{m}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{m-1}]
for all
m\leq n.

In this goal we first notice that for all
m\leq k
, by triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|D_{m}\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{j>k}\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{j})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j>k}\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{j})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle=(n-k)\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]\Big\|_{p},

where the last equality is due to the fact that
(Y_{i})
are identically distributed.
Moreover this can be further bounded by noticing that

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|\mathcal{F}_{m}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{n})^{2}|Y_{m}]^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\mathbb{E}\Big[(X_{m}^{2}+\sigma^{2})^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\sigma^{2/p}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}},

where step (a) exploits the assumption that the random variables
(Y_{i})
are positive, ensuring
|Y_{m}-Y_{n}|\overset{a.s.}{\leq}R
. Step
(b)
holds because

\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{n})^{2}|Y_{m}]=(Y_{m}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{m}])^{2}+\mathbb{E}[(Y_{n}-\mathbb{E}[Y_{m}])^{2}|Y_{m}]=X_{m}^{2}+\mathrm{Var}(Y_{m})=X_{m}^{2}+\sigma^{2}.

Step
(c)
is due to the fact that

\mathbb{E}[(X_{m}^{2}+\sigma^{2})^{2}]=\mathbb{E}[X_{m}^{4}+2\sigma^{2}X_{m}^{2}+\sigma^{4}]\leq R^{2}\mathbb{E}[X_{m}^{2}]+2\sigma^{2}\mathrm{Var}(X_{m})+\sigma^{4}=\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2}).

Hence we obtain that for
m\leq k
,

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]\Big\|_{p}\leq R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}

(22)

and so that

\displaystyle\Big\|D_{m}\Big\|_{p}\leq(n-k)R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}.

(23)

Now if
m>k
, we have

\displaystyle\|D_{m}\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{i\leq k}(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq k\Big\|(Y_{1}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{1}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{1}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq k\Big(\Big\|(Y_{1}-Y_{m})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}+\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{1}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{1}]\Big\|_{p}\Big)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}kR^{\ell-2/p}\mathbb{E}[(Y_{1}-Y_{m})^{2}]^{\frac{1}{p}}+k\|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}|Y_{1}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}2^{\frac{1}{p}}k\sigma^{2/p}R^{\ell-2/p}+kR^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle=k\sigma^{2/p}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\Big(2^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\frac{2}{p}}+(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}\Big),

where again to get
(a)
we used the fact that
|Y_{1}-Y_{m}|\overset{a.s}{\leq}R
and to get
(b)
we used the fact that
\mathbb{E}[(Y_{1}-Y_{m})^{2}]=2\mathrm{Var}(X_{1})=2\sigma^{2}
and
eq.

22

In addition, for
m>k
, the following bound also holds:

\displaystyle\|D_{m}\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{i\leq k}(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq k\|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]\|_{p}+\Big\|\sum_{i\leq k}(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq kR^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Big\|\sum_{i\leq k}(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]\Big\|_{p}.

Note that conditionally on
Y_{m}
, the random variables
(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]
,
i=1,\dots,k
are centered i.i.d. random variables. Hence we obtain that got
m>k
,

\displaystyle\|D_{m}\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq kR^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Big\|\sum_{i\leq k}(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq kR^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Big\|\sum_{i\leq k}\mathbb{E}\big[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-(Y_{n+1}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-(Y_{i}-Y_{n+1})^{\ell}|Y_{i},Y_{m}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}kR^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}+\sqrt{k}R^{\ell}\min\{\sqrt{p-1},A_{p}+A^{*}_{k,p}\},

where to obtain
(a)
we used
lemma

K.2
together with the fact that, since
Y_{i}-Y_{m}=(Y_{i}-Y_{n+1})+(Y_{n+1}-Y_{m})
, for
\ell
odd, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\Big|(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-(Y_{n+1}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-(Y_{i}-Y_{n+1})^{\ell}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\max\{|Y_{i}-Y_{m}|^{\ell},|Y_{n+1}-Y_{m}|^{\ell},|Y_{i}-Y_{n+1}|^{\ell}\}

\displaystyle\leq R^{\ell}.

Hence by Theorem 2.1 of
[
45
]
(
lemma

K.3
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{p-1}\sqrt{k(n-k)}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}\Big((n-k)(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}+k\min\Big\{(2^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\frac{2}{p}}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}})^{2},\penalty\ \Big((R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\penalty\ +\frac{R^{\frac{4}{p}}}{\sqrt{k}\sigma^{2/p}}\min\{\sqrt{p-1},A_{p}+A^{*}_{k,p}\}\Big)^{2}\Big\}\Big)^{1/2}.

If
p\geq 4
we derive an alternative bound using
[
40
]
.
In this goal we remark that if
m\leq k
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[D_{m}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{m-1}]

\displaystyle=(n-k)^{2}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell-4}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{n})^{2}|Y_{m}]^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell-4}\mathbb{E}[(\sigma^{2}+X_{m}^{2})^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell-4}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2}).

which implies that

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[D_{m}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{m-1}]\|_{p/2}

\displaystyle\leq(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell-4}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2}).

(24)

Moreover, if, instead, we have
m>k
, then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[D_{m}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{m-1}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\sum_{i\leq k}(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]\Big)^{2}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{m-1}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq k\sum_{i\leq k}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big((Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]\Big)^{2}\Big|Y_{i}\Big]

\displaystyle=k\sum_{i\leq k}\mathbb{E}\Big[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{2\ell}|Y_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq kR^{2\ell-2}\sum_{i\leq k}\mathbb{E}\Big[(Y_{i}-Y_{m})^{2}\Big|Y_{i}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq kR^{2\ell-2}\sum_{i\leq k}(\sigma^{2}+X_{i}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq R^{2\ell-2}(k\sigma^{2}+\sum_{i\leq k}X_{i}^{2}).

Hence we obtain that

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[D_{m}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{m-1}]\|_{p/2}

\displaystyle\leq R^{2\ell-2}k^{2}\sigma^{2}+k^{2}R^{2\ell-4/p}\sigma^{4/p}.

Hence it follows that for
p\geq 4
, the following inequality also holds:

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}((W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n}))\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}C_{p}\bigg\{R^{\ell-2}\sqrt{(n-k)k}\sqrt{(n-k)\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})+k{(R^{2}\sigma^{2}+R^{4-\frac{4}{p}})}}

\displaystyle+k^{\frac{1}{p}}(n-k)^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\bigg[k^{p-1}\Big(\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{1/p}+2^{1/p}k^{-\frac{1}{2}}R^{2/p}\Big)^{p}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(n-k)^{p-1}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})\bigg]^{\frac{1}{p}}\bigg\}.

∎

Lemma C.11
.

For all even
\ell\geq 4
the following holds for
p>2
:

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}R^{\ell-2}\bigg[\sqrt{p-1}R^{2-\frac{4}{p}}\Big((n-k)\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+kR^{\frac{2}{p}}\min\Big\{(\frac{1}{4})^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\frac{2}{p}},2^{\frac{1}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}+R^{-2/p}\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}\Big\}^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+{2\sqrt{k(n-k)}\sigma^{2}}\bigg].

Moreover, for
p\geq 4
, the following inequality also holds:

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}C_{p}\Big\{R^{\ell-2}\sqrt{(n-k)k}\sqrt{2(n-k)\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}+(n-k)R^{2}+k(\sigma^{2}+\sigma^{4/p}R^{2-\frac{4}{p}})}

\displaystyle+k^{\frac{1}{p}}(n-k)^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\Big((n-k)^{p-1}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})+k^{p-1}\min\big\{\frac{R^{4}}{4},R^{2}\sigma^{2}(2^{1/p}+(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{1/p})^{p}\big\}\Big)^{1/p}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}k(n-k)R^{\ell-2}\sigma^{2}.

Alternatively the following bound also holds

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\|\mathbb{E}((W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n}))\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}}{n}\Big\{kR^{\ell}\|\mathrm{binom}(k,\frac{2\sigma^{2}}{R^{2}})\|_{p}+(n-k)k2^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\ell-2/p}\sigma^{2/p}\Big\}.

Proof.

First, note that by the definition of
W_{k}^{\prime}
, the following holds

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\|\mathbb{E}((W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n}))\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle s^{-1}\frac{1}{k(n-k)}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(X_{i}-X_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(X_{i}-X_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}.

As
\ell
is even, we have

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}\Big((Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big)\Big\|_{p}+k(n-k)\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}\Big((Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big)\Big\|_{p}+2k(n-k)R^{\ell-2}\sigma^{2},

where
\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}((Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}])\|_{p}
can be again bounded using the Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund type inequality and Theorem 1 of
[
40
]
.
Again, in this goal, we define the following filtration

\mathcal{F}_{i}=\sigma(Y_{1},\dots,Y_{i}),

and

D_{i}=\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{m}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{i}\Big]-\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{m}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}\Big].

By a telescopic sum argument we notice that

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}\Big((Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big)=\sum_{m\leq n}D_{m}.

For
i\leq k
, we have

\displaystyle\|D_{i}\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{j>k}\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq(n-k)\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{n}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle=(n-k)\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{n}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big|^{p}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}(n-k)R^{\ell-2\ell/p}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{n}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big)^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}},

where to get
(a)
we used the fact that
|Y_{i}-Y_{n}|\overset{a.s}{\leq}R
.
Note that we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{n}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]^{2}\Big]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{n}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R^{2\ell-4}\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{2}|Y_{i}]^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R^{2\ell-4}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2}),

where
(a)
is because
\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{2}|Y_{i}]^{2}\Big]\leq\mathbb{E}[(X_{i}^{2}+\sigma^{2})^{2}]\leq\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})
. Therefore,

\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{n}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big)^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}.

Hence it follows that for
i\leq k
,

\|D_{i}\|_{p}{\leq}(n-k)R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}.

Now if
i>k
, by a triangle inequality argument we obtain that

\displaystyle\|D_{i}\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\sum_{j<k}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}|Y_{j}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq k\Big\|(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}|Y_{1}]\Big\|_{p},

where we have

\displaystyle\Big\|(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}|Y_{1}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}|Y_{1}]\Big|^{p}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle R^{\ell-2\ell/p}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big((Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}|Y_{1}]\Big)^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle(\frac{1}{4})^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\ell},

where
(a)
is by
|Y_{i}-Y_{i}|\leq R
, and
(b)
is by Popoviciu’s inequality.
Alternatively, we have

\displaystyle\Big\|(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}|Y_{1}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}\Big\|_{p}+\Big\|\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{1})^{\ell}|Y_{1}]\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle{\leq}

\displaystyle 2^{\frac{1}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}R^{\ell-2/p}+R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}.

Hence it follows that, for
i>k
,

\|D_{i}\|_{p}{\leq}kR^{\ell-\frac{2}{p}}\min\Big\{R^{\frac{2}{p}}/4^{\frac{1}{p}},2^{\frac{1}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}+R^{-2/p}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}\Big\}.

Hence by Theorem 2.1 of
[
45
]
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}]\Big\|^{2}_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(p-1)\Big(k\Big((n-k)R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle+(n-k)\Big(kR^{\ell-\frac{2}{p}}\min\Big\{(\frac{1}{4})^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\frac{2}{p}},2^{\frac{1}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}+R^{-2/p}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}\Big\}\Big)^{2}\Big).

Therefore, it follows that

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\|\mathbb{E}((W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}

\displaystyle|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n}))\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}R^{\ell-2}\Big[

\displaystyle\sqrt{p-1}R^{2-\frac{4}{p}}\Big((n-k)\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+kR^{\frac{2}{p}}\min\Big\{(\frac{1}{4})^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\frac{2}{p}},2^{\frac{1}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}+R^{-2/p}\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}\Big\}^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+{2\sqrt{k(n-k)}\sigma^{2}}\Big].

Moreover if
p\geq 4
then we can alternatively use Theorem 1 of
[
40
]
to bound
\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}((Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}])\|_{p}
. In this goal we first note that if
i\leq k
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[D_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]

\displaystyle=(n-k)^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}]\Big)^{2}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}(n-k)^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]^{2}+\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}]^{2}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell-4}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}[(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{2}|Y_{m}]^{2}]+(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell}

\displaystyle=(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell-4}\mathbb{E}[(\sigma^{2}+X_{i}^{2})^{2}]+(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell}

\displaystyle\leq(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell-4}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})+(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell},

where
(a)
is due to the fact that
(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{\ell}\geq 0.
Hence we have that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}[D_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\big\|_{p/2}\leq(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell-4}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})+(n-k)^{2}R^{2\ell}.

If
i>k
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[D_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\sum_{m\leq k}(Y_{m}-Y_{i})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{i})^{\ell}|Y_{i}]\Big)^{2}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq k\sum_{m\leq k}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big((Y_{m}-Y_{i})^{\ell}-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{i})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]\Big)^{2}\Big|Y_{m}\Big]

\displaystyle=k\sum_{m\leq k}\mathbb{E}\big[(Y_{m}-Y_{i})^{2\ell}|Y_{m}]-\mathbb{E}[(Y_{m}-Y_{i})^{\ell}|Y_{m}]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq kR^{2\ell-2}\sum_{m\leq k}\mathbb{E}\Big[(Y_{m}-Y_{i})^{2}\Big|Y_{m}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq kR^{2\ell-2}\sum_{i\leq k}(\sigma^{2}+X_{i}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq kR^{2\ell-2}(k\sigma^{2}+\sum_{i\leq k}X_{i}^{2}).

Hence we obtain that

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[D_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\|_{p/2}

\displaystyle\leq R^{2\ell-2}k^{2}\sigma^{2}+k^{2}R^{2\ell-4/p}\sigma^{4/p}.

(25)

Hence using Theorem 1 of
[
40
]
the following alternative bound is also valid according to

lemma

K.6

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{m\leq n}D_{m}\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq C_{p}\Big\{R^{\ell-2}\sqrt{(n-k)k}\sqrt{2(n-k)\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}+(n-k)R^{2}+k(\sigma^{2}+\sigma^{4/p}R^{2-\frac{4}{p}})}

\displaystyle+k^{\frac{1}{p}}(n-k)^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\Big((n-k)^{p-1}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})+k^{p-1}\min\big\{\frac{R^{4}}{4},R^{2}\sigma^{2}(2^{1/p}+(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{1/p})^{p}\big\}\Big)^{1/p}\Big\}.

Therefore it follows, for
p\geq 4
, that the following inequality also holds:

\displaystyle s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\|\mathbb{E}((W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n}))\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}C_{p}\Big\{R^{\ell-2}\sqrt{(n-k)k}\sqrt{2(n-k)\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}+(n-k)R^{2}+k(\sigma^{2}+\sigma^{4/p}R^{2-\frac{4}{p}})}

\displaystyle+k^{\frac{1}{p}}(n-k)^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\Big((n-k)^{p-1}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})+k^{p-1}\min\big\{\frac{R^{4}}{4},R^{2}\sigma^{2}(2^{1/p}+(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{1/p})^{p}\big\}\Big)^{1/p}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}k(n-k)R^{\ell-2}\sigma^{2}.

We hence established the first two bounds of
lemma

C.11
. Moreover
if
k\leq n/2
we remark that the following also holds

\displaystyle\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\leq k\\
k<j\leq n\end{subarray}}(Y_{i}-Y_{j})^{\ell}\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq k\|\sum_{i\leq k}(Y_{i}-Y_{k+i})^{\ell}\|_{p}+(n-k)k\|(Y_{i}-Y_{n})^{l}\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq k\|\sum_{i\leq k}(Y_{i}-Y_{k+i})^{\ell}\|_{p}+(n-k)k2^{\frac{1}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}R^{\ell-2/p}.

Now note that the random variables
((Y_{i}-Y_{k+i})^{\ell})_{i\leq k}
are i.i.d. positive random variables. Moreover we remark that
\mathbb{E}((Y_{i}-Y_{k+i})^{\ell})\leq 2R^{\ell-2}\sigma^{2}
and
\mathbb{E}((Y_{i}-Y_{k+i})^{\ell p})\leq 2R^{\ell p-2}\sigma^{2}
. Therefore using
lemma

K.7
in
[
2
]
, we obtain that

\|\sum_{i\leq k}(Y_{i}-Y_{k+i})^{\ell}\|_{p}\leq R^{\ell}\|\mathrm{binom}(k,\frac{2\sigma^{2}}{R^{2}})\|_{p}.

Therefore, we have

s^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\|\mathbb{E}((W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n}))\|_{p}\leq\frac{1}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big(kR^{\ell}\|\mathrm{binom}(k,\frac{2\sigma^{2}}{R^{2}})\|_{p}+(n-k)k2^{\frac{1}{p}}]\sigma^{2/p}R^{\ell-2/p}\Big).

∎

Lemma C.12
.

For every odd integer
\ell\geq 3
, let
S_{k,\kappa}:=\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}
and
\tilde{S}_{k}:=\frac{R^{2}n}{k\sigma^{2}}
. Then for any integer
K_{p}\geq 1
, the following holds:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 3\\
\mathrm{odd}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}R^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(n-k)}\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{m!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}R\tilde{S}_{k}^{m}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+1)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m}}{2^{m}(2K+3)}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}}{2(2K_{p}+3)(n-k)}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy.

Proof.

First, we note that

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 3\\
\mathrm{odd}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}R^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}dt

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 3\\
\mathrm{odd}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\ell!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\|H_{l-1}\|_{p}R^{\ell}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{\ell-1}}{(\sqrt{1-x^{2}})^{\ell-1}}dx

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{m\geq 1}\frac{1}{(2m+1)!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+1)}\|H_{2m}\|_{p}R^{2m+1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m}}{(1-x^{2})^{m}}dx,

where
(a)
is obtained by using a change of variables
x=e^{-t}
and
(b)
by writing
\ell=2m+1
. Using the fact that, according to
[
46
]
, the following holds:

\displaystyle\sqrt{(2m)!}

\displaystyle\geq\sqrt{\sqrt{2\pi(2m)}\cdot(2m/e)^{2m}\cdot\exp\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{12(2m)+1}}}\right)}

\displaystyle\geq e^{-19/300}2^{m}m!/(\pi m)^{1/4},

and by using
lemma

K.5
, we obtain that for all
K_{p}\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 3\\
\mathrm{odd}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}R^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{(2m+1)!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+1)}\|H_{2m}\|_{p}R^{2m+1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m}}{(1-x^{2})^{m}}dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{m\geq K_{p}+1}\frac{1}{(2m+1)\sqrt{(2m)!}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+1)}(p-1)^{m}R^{2m+1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m}}{(1-x^{2})^{m}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{(2m+1)!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+1)}\|H_{2m}\|_{p}R^{2m+1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m}}{(1-x^{2})^{m}}dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{m\geq K_{p}+1}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}m^{1/4}}{2^{m}m!(2m+1)}\sqrt{\frac{n}{(n-k)k}}^{2m+1}(p-1)^{m}R^{2m+1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m}}{(1-x^{2})^{m}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{m!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+1)}R^{2m+1}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+1)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m}}{2^{m}(2K_{p}+3)}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m}}{(1-x^{2})^{m}}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}}{(2K_{p}+3)}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\Big[e^{\frac{x^{2}(p-1)R^{2}}{2(1-x^{2})\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}-1\Big]dx.

Now by change of variable,
y^{-1}=(n-k)(1-x^{2})
we obtain the desired result:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 3\\
\mathrm{odd}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}R^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(n-k)}\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{m!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}R^{2m+1}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+1)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m}}{2^{m}(2K_{p}+3)}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{n^{m}}{\sigma^{2m}k^{m}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}}{2(n-k)(2K_{p}+3)}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)R^{2}n}{2k\sigma^{2}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy.

∎

Lemma C.13
.

For all
\ell\geq 4
even integer, if we denote
S_{k,\kappa}:=\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}
and
\tilde{S}_{k}:=\frac{R^{2}n}{k\sigma^{2}}
, choose
K_{p}\geq 1
then the following holds

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 4\\
\mathrm{even}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}R^{\ell-1}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{\tilde{S}_{k}^{m}}{m!}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m+1}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+2)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m+1/2}}{2^{m}(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{2(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy.

Proof.

First, we note that

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 4\\
\mathrm{even}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}R^{\ell-1}dt

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 4\\
\mathrm{even}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}R^{\ell-1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{\ell-1}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-x^{2}})^{\ell-1}}dx

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{m\geq 1}\frac{1}{(2m+2)!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+2)}\|H_{2m+1}\|_{p}R^{2m+1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m+1}}{(1-x^{2})^{m+\frac{1}{2}}}dx,

where (a) is due to a change of variable
x=e^{-t}
, (b) is due to the fact that any even integer
\ell\geq 4
can be rewritten as
2m+2
for
m\geq 1
and finally where (c) is due to a change of variable
y=(n-k)(1-x^{2})
. We then remark that according to
[
46
]
, we have

\sqrt{(2m+1)!}=\sqrt{2m+1}\sqrt{2m!}\geq\sqrt{2m+1}e^{-19/300}2^{m}m!/(\pi m)^{1/4}.

Moreover, by
lemma

K.5
, the following inequality for the Hermite polynomials holds

\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|H_{2m+1}}}\right\|_{p}\leq\sqrt{(2m+1)!}\sqrt{p-1}^{2m+1}.

Hence for all
K_{p}\geq 1
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 4\\
\mathrm{even}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}R^{\ell-1}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{(2m+2)!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+2)}\|H_{2m+1}\|_{p}R^{2m+1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m+1}}{(1-x^{2})^{m+\frac{1}{2}}}dx

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{m\geq K_{p}+1}\frac{1}{(2m+2)\sqrt{(2m+1)!}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+2)}(p-1)^{m+1/2}R^{2m+1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m+1}}{(1-x^{2})^{m+\frac{1}{2}}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{(2m+2)!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+2)}\|H_{2m+1}\|_{p}R^{2m+1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m+1}}{(1-x^{2})^{m+\frac{1}{2}}}dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{m\geq K_{p}+1}\frac{m^{1/4}e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(p-1)^{m+1/2}R^{2m+1}}{2^{m}m!(2m+2)\sqrt{2m+1}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+2)}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m+1}}{(1-x^{2})^{m+\frac{1}{2}}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{m!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-(2m+2)}R^{2m+1}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m+1}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+2)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m+1/2}}{2^{m}(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x^{2m+1}}{(1-x^{2})^{m+\frac{1}{2}}}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}\sigma_{n,k}^{-2}R\sqrt{p-1}}{(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}}}\frac{x}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}}}\Big[e^{\frac{x^{2}(p-1)R^{2}}{2(1-x^{2})\sigma_{n,k}}}-1\Big]dx.

Finally, by the change of variable
y^{-1}=(n-k)(1-x^{2})
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 4\\
\mathrm{even}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}R^{\ell-1}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{\tilde{S}_{k}^{m}}{m!}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m+1}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+2)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m+1/2}}{2^{m}(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{2(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy.

∎

Now we may use
lemmas

C.10
,

,

and

to bound the term
(a_{3})
. In this goal, we define

C_{\mathrm{odd}}:=\begin{cases}C_{\mathrm{odd},1}&\text{if }p<4,\\
\min\{C_{\mathrm{odd},1},C_{\mathrm{odd},2}\}&\text{if }p\geq 4,\end{cases}

(26)

where

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{odd},1}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{p-1}\sqrt{k(n-k)}R^{-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}\bigg((n-k)(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}+k\min\Big\{(2^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\frac{2}{p}}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}})^{2},

\displaystyle\penalty\ \Big((R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}\penalty\ +\frac{R^{\frac{4}{p}}}{\sqrt{k}\sigma^{2/p}}\min\{\sqrt{p-1},A_{p}+A^{*}_{k,p}\}\Big)^{2}\Big\}\bigg)^{1/2},

and

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{odd},2}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}C_{p}\bigg\{R^{-2}\sqrt{(n-k)k}\sqrt{(n-k)\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})+k{(R^{2}\sigma^{2}+R^{4-\frac{4}{p}})}}

\displaystyle+k^{\frac{1}{p}}(n-k)^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\bigg(k^{p-1}\Big(\sigma^{2/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{1/p}+2^{1/p}k^{-\frac{1}{2}}R^{2/p}\Big)^{p}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(n-k)^{p-1}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})\bigg)^{\frac{1}{p}}\bigg\},

and

C_{\mathrm{even}}:=\begin{cases}\min\{C_{\mathrm{even},1},C_{\mathrm{even},3}\}&\text{if }p<4,\\
\min\{C_{\mathrm{even},1},C_{\mathrm{even},2},C_{\mathrm{even},3}\}&\text{if }p\geq 4,\end{cases}

(27)

where

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{even},1}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}R^{-2}\bigg[\sqrt{p-1}R^{2-\frac{4}{p}}\Big((n-k)\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+kR^{\frac{2}{p}}\min\Big\{(\frac{1}{4})^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\frac{2}{p}},2^{\frac{1}{p}}\sigma^{\frac{2}{p}}+R^{-2/p}\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}}\Big\}^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+{2\sqrt{k(n-k)}\sigma^{2}}\bigg],

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{even},2}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}C_{p}\Big\{R^{-2}\sqrt{(n-k)k}\sqrt{2(n-k)\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}+(n-k)R^{2}+k(\sigma^{2}+\sigma^{4/p}R^{2-\frac{4}{p}})}

\displaystyle+k^{\frac{1}{p}}(n-k)^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\ell-\frac{4}{p}}\Big((n-k)^{p-1}\sigma^{2}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})+k^{p-1}\min\big\{\frac{R^{4}}{4},R^{2}\sigma^{2}(2^{1/p}+(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{1/p})^{p}\big\}\Big)^{1/p}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{n}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}k(n-k)R^{\ell-2}\sigma^{2},

and

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{even},3}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\Big\{k\|\mathrm{binom}(k,\frac{2\sigma^{2}}{R^{2}})\|_{p}+(n-k)k2^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{-2/p}\sigma^{2/p}\Big\}.

Lemma C.14
.

The following bound holds for all

:

\displaystyle(a_{3})\leq

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}}{2(2K_{p}+3)(n-k)}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy\bigg\}

\displaystyle+C_{\mathrm{even}}R\bigg\{\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{\tilde{S}_{k}^{m}}{m!}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m+1}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+2)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m+1/2}}{2^{m}(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{2(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy\bigg\}.

Proof.

We have that

\displaystyle(a_{3})=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\geq 3}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-lt}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{s\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}\big[(W_{k}^{\prime}-W_{k})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})\big]\Big\|_{p}dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 3\\
\mathrm{odd}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{s}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}\big[(W_{k}^{\prime}-W_{k})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})\big]\Big\|_{p}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}dt

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 4\\
\mathrm{even}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{s}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}\big[(W_{k}^{\prime}-W_{k})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})\big]\Big\|_{p}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}dt.

By
lemma

C.10
, we have that for odd

,

\frac{1}{s}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}\big[(W_{k}^{\prime}-W_{k})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})\big]\Big\|_{p}\leq C_{\mathrm{odd}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}R^{\ell},

and by

C.11
, we have that for even

,

\frac{1}{s}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}\big[(W_{k}^{\prime}-W_{k})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})\big]\Big\|_{p}\leq C_{\mathrm{even}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}R^{\ell},

where
C_{\mathrm{odd}}
and
C_{\mathrm{even}}
are defined as in
eq.

26
and
eq.

27
.
Hence we have

\displaystyle(a_{3})\leq

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{odd}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 3\\
\mathrm{odd}\end{subarray}}R^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}dt

\displaystyle+C_{\mathrm{even}}R\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 4\\
\mathrm{even}\end{subarray}}R^{\ell-1}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}dt.

Note that by
lemma

C.12
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 3\\
\mathrm{odd}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}R^{\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(n-k)}\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{m!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}R\tilde{S}_{k}^{m}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+1)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m}}{2^{m}(2K_{p}+3)}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}}{2(2K_{p}+3)(n-k)}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy.

Also note that by

C.13
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell\geq 4\\
\mathrm{even}\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-\ell t}\|H_{\ell-1}\|_{p}}{\ell!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{\ell-1}}R^{\ell-1}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\sum_{K_{p}\geq m\geq 1}\frac{\tilde{S}_{k}^{m}}{m!}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m+1}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+2)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m+1/2}}{2^{m}(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K_{p}+1)^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{2(2K_{p}+4)\sqrt{2K_{p}+3}}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy.

∎

Theorem C.15
.

For
p\geq 2
, the following inequality holds for any
K\geq 0
:

\Big\|\mathcal{W}_{p}(W_{k},\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{n,k}^{2})|\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n}))\Big\|_{p}\leq\omega^{R}_{p}(n,k,\sigma),

where we set

\displaystyle\omega^{R}_{p}(n,k,\sigma):=\min\Big(\sqrt{p-1}\sigma_{n,k}(1+2\sigma^{-1}),

\displaystyle\penalty\ \Omega^{R}_{p}(n,k,\sigma)\Big),

(28)

where we have set

\displaystyle\Omega^{R}_{p}(n,k,\sigma)

\displaystyle=\inf_{\begin{subarray}{c}\kappa\leq R^{2}/\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\\
K\geq 1\end{subarray}}\sigma_{n,k}\Bigg\{\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\mathcal{S}^{p}_{\sigma,R}(n,k)\times(1-\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}})

\displaystyle+\sqrt{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\Gamma((p+1)/2)}{\Gamma(1/2)}}}\right)^{1/p}\arccos{(\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}})}

\displaystyle+\|H_{1}\|_{p}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\min\Big(\sqrt{p-1}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p},A_{p}\sqrt{\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1}+A^{*}_{n,p}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\min\Big(\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p},A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{k,p}\Big)^{2}\Big]\Big(1-\frac{R}{\sigma_{n,k}\sqrt{\kappa}}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{\mathrm{odd}}\bigg\{\frac{1}{2(n-k)}\sum_{K\geq m\geq 1}\frac{1}{m!}\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}R\tilde{S}_{k}^{m}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+1)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m}}{2^{m}(2K+3)}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}(K+1)^{1/4}}{2(2K+3)(n-k)}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy\bigg\}

\displaystyle+C_{\mathrm{even}}R\bigg\{\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\sum_{K\geq m\geq 1}\frac{\tilde{S}_{k}^{m}}{m!}\Big(\frac{\|H_{2m+1}\|_{p}m!}{(2m+2)!}-\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K+1)^{1/4}(p-1)^{m+1/2}}{2^{m}(2K+4)\sqrt{2K+3}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})^{m}dy

\displaystyle+\frac{Rn}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}(K+1)^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{2(2K+4)\sqrt{2K+3}}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy\Big\}\Bigg\}.

where
\mathcal{S}^{p}_{\sigma,R}(n,k)
is defined in
lemma

I.1
.

Proof.

Recall that in
eq.

18
, we have established that

\displaystyle\sigma^{-1}_{n,k}\Big\|\mathcal{W}_{p}(W_{k},\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{n,k}^{2})|\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n}))\Big\|_{p}

(29)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big|e^{-t}\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}W_{k}-\frac{e^{-2t}}{\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}}}\tilde{Z}\Big|^{p}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}dt

\displaystyle+\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}e^{-t}\Big\|\frac{1}{s}\mathbb{E}\big[W_{k}-W_{k}^{\prime}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})\big]-W_{k}\Big\|_{p}dt

\displaystyle+\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-2t}\|H_{1}\|_{p}}{\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}}}\Big\|\frac{1}{2s}\sigma_{n,k}^{-2}\mathbb{E}\big[(W^{\prime}_{k}-W_{k})^{2}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})\big]-1\Big\|_{p}dt

\displaystyle+\sum_{l\geq 3}\int_{-\frac{1}{2}\log(1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}})}^{\infty}\frac{e^{-lt}\|H_{l-1}\|_{p}}{sl!(\sqrt{1-e^{-2t}})^{l-1}}\sigma_{n,k}^{-\ell}\Big\|\mathbb{E}\big[(W_{k}^{\prime}-W_{k})^{\ell}|W_{k},\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n})\big]\Big\|_{p}dt

\displaystyle:=(a_{0})+(a_{1})+(a_{2})+(a_{3}).

Combining the result of

C.5
,
C.7
,
C.9
and
C.14
and reorganizing terms gives us the first bound. Secondly, we remark that by a triangle inequality we also obtain the following trivial bound:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{W}_{p}(W_{k},\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{n,k}^{2})|\mathcal{U}(X_{1:n}))\Big\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\|W_{k}\|_{p}+\sigma_{n,k}\|Z\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{k}{n})\|S_{k}\|_{p}+\frac{k}{n}\|S_{n}-S_{k}\|_{p}+\sigma_{n,k}\|Z\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{p-1}\Big(\frac{n-k}{n}\sqrt{k}+\frac{k\sqrt{n-k}}{{n}}\Big)R+\sqrt{p-1}\sigma_{n,k}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{p-1}\frac{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}{\sqrt{n}}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{n-k}}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{k}}{\sqrt{n}}\Big)R+\sqrt{p-1}\sigma_{n,k}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{p-1}\sigma_{n,k}(1+\sqrt{2}R\sigma^{-1}).

∎

C.3
Proof of Corollary
5.2.1

We define the following constant

\displaystyle\tilde{K}_{R,\sigma}:=

\displaystyle\Big\{\hyperref@@ii[KRsig]{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{K_{R,\sigma}}}+\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma}(1+\sigma/2)+2^{\frac{3}{p}}\sigma^{2/p-1}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{3}\frac{R^{2}}{2}\Big(e^{R^{2}}-1\Big)\log\Big(\frac{1+\sqrt{1/2}}{1-\sqrt{1/2}}\Big)

(30)

\displaystyle\quad+R^{-\frac{4}{p}}\bigg[\tilde{R}^{2/p}4^{1/p}\sigma^{-1}+{2}\bigg]\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{8\sqrt{3}}\frac{R^{5/2}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big(e^{R^{2}}-1\Big)\Big\}\vee(1+\sqrt{2}R\sigma^{-1}),

(31)

R
,

is defined in

.

Lemma C.16
.

Let
R>0
and
R/2>\sigma>0
. Then for all
k,n\in\mathbb{N}
and all
p\in\mathbb{N}
, we have that

\omega^{R}_{p}(n,k,\sigma)\leq\tilde{K}_{R,\sigma}p.

Moreover, for all
0<\sigma_{1}<\sigma_{2}\leq\frac{R}{2}
, we have

\max_{\sigma\in[\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}]}|\tilde{K}_{R,\sigma}|<\infty.

Proof.

We will establish the bound in two steps: First for the case
p\leq\sigma_{n,k}^{2}+1
, and then for
p>\sigma_{n,k}^{2}+1
.

In the first case, we use the bound established in
theorem

C.15
with
K=0
and
\kappa=C/(p-1)
with
C=2R^{2}
, which tells us that

\displaystyle\Omega^{R}_{p}(n,k,\sigma)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}^{p}_{\sigma,R}(n,k)\times(1-\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}})+\sigma_{n,k}\Bigg\{\sqrt{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\Gamma((p+1)/2)}{\Gamma(1/2)}}}\right)^{1/p}\arccos{(\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}})}

\displaystyle+\|H_{1}\|_{p}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\min\Big(\sqrt{p-1}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p},A_{p}\sqrt{\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1}+A^{*}_{n,p}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\min\Big(\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p},A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{k,p}\Big)^{2}\Big]\Big(1-\frac{R}{\sigma_{n,k}\sqrt{\kappa}}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{\mathrm{odd}}\bigg\{\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}}{6(n-k)}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy\bigg\}

\displaystyle+C_{\mathrm{even}}\bigg\{\frac{R^{2}n}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{8\sqrt{3}}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy\Big\}\Bigg\}.

We will bound each term successively. In this goal, we first remark that
using
lemma

I.2
,

\mathcal{S}_{p}^{\sigma,R}(n,k)(1-\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}})\leq\mathcal{S}_{p}^{\sigma,R}(n,k)\leq\hyperref@@ii[KRsig]{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{K_{R,\sigma}}}p,

where
K

R
,
σ

is a constant defined in
lemma

I.2
. Moreover, using the mean value theorem, we observe that

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{\Gamma((p+1)/2)}{\Gamma(1/2)}}}\right)^{1/p}\arccos{(\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\kappa}})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{p-1}\arccos{(\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\kappa}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{p-1}\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\kappa\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\kappa}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{p-1}\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\kappa\sqrt{0.5}},

where for the last inequality we used the fact that

\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\kappa}\leq 2.

Moreover using
lemma

K.5
, we note that
\|H_{1}\|_{p}\leq\sqrt{p-1}
. This directly implies that

\displaystyle\|H_{1}\|_{p}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p},A_{p}\sqrt{\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1}+A^{*}_{n,p}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p}\Big\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\min\Big\{\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p},A_{p}+\tilde{R}_{s}^{1-2/p}A^{*}_{k,p}\Big\}^{2}\Big]\Big(1-\frac{R}{\sigma_{n,k}\sqrt{\kappa}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq(p-1)\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}\Big[\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{R}^{2}_{s}-1)^{1-1/p}+\frac{\sqrt{p-1}}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{R}_{s}^{2-\frac{4}{p}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq(p-1)\frac{\tilde{R}^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}\Big[1+\frac{\sqrt{p-1}}{\sqrt{n}}\Big].

Moreover, we note that

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{odd}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{p-1}\sqrt{k(n-k)}R^{-\frac{4}{p}}\sigma^{2/p}\bigg((n-k)(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}+k(2^{\frac{1}{p}}R^{\frac{2}{p}}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{1}{p}})^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{p-1}R^{-\frac{4}{p}}2^{\frac{3}{p}}R^{4/p}\sigma^{2/p}.

Now, note that for all
x\in[0,\sqrt{1-\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\kappa}}]
, we have
\frac{1}{(1-x^{2})^{m}}\leq\Big(\frac{\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\kappa}{R^{2}}\Big)^{m-1}\frac{1}{1-x^{2}}.
Hence, a change of variables implies

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}}{6(n-k)}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}\sqrt{y-\frac{1}{n-k}}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}}{6(n-k)}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}2(n-k)\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}\frac{x^{2}}{(n-k)(1-x^{2})}}-1\Big]dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}}{3}\sum_{m\geq 1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{m!}\frac{(p-1)^{m}}{2^{m}}S_{k,\kappa}^{m}\kappa^{m}\frac{x^{2m}}{(1-x^{2})^{m}}dx

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}R\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}\pi^{1/4}}{3}S_{k,\kappa}\sum_{m\geq 1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{m!}\frac{(p-1)^{m}}{2^{m}}\kappa^{m}\frac{x^{2m}}{1-x^{2}}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}R^{3}\sigma_{n,k}^{-3}\pi^{1/4}}{3\kappa}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}\Big(e^{\frac{(p-1)\kappa x^{2}}{2}}-1\Big)\frac{1}{1-x^{2}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{3}\frac{\sigma_{n,k}^{-3}R^{3}}{\kappa}\Big(e^{\frac{1}{2}(p-1)\kappa(1-S_{k,\kappa})}-1\Big)\log\Big(\frac{1+\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}{1-\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{3}\frac{\sigma_{n,k}^{-3}R^{3}}{\kappa}\Big(e^{\frac{C}{2}(1-S_{k,\kappa})}-1\Big)\log\Big(\frac{1+\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}{1-\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{3}\frac{\sigma_{n,k}^{-3}R^{3}(p-1)}{C}\Big(e^{\frac{C}{2}(1-S_{k,\kappa})}-1\Big)\log\Big(\frac{1+\sqrt{1/2}}{1-\sqrt{1/2}}\Big).

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{6}{\sigma_{n,k}^{-3}R(p-1)}{}\Big(e^{\frac{C}{2}}-1\Big)\log\Big(\frac{1+\sqrt{1/2}}{1-\sqrt{1/2}}\Big).

Moreover, we remark that

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{even}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}R^{-\frac{4}{p}}{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}\bigg[\sqrt{p-1}\Big((n-k)\sigma^{4/p}(R^{2}+3\sigma^{2})^{\frac{2}{p}}+kR^{\frac{2}{p}}(\frac{1}{4})^{\frac{2}{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+{2\sqrt{k(n-k)}\sigma^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq R^{-\frac{4}{p}}\bigg[\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}^{2/p}4^{1/p}\frac{{\sqrt{k(n-k)}}}{\sqrt{n}}+{2}\sigma_{n,k}^{2}\bigg].

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\frac{R^{2}n}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{8\sqrt{3}}\int_{\frac{1}{(n-k)}}^{\frac{1}{(n-k)S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{y^{3/2}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}(y-\frac{1}{n-k})}-1\Big]dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{R^{2}n}{2\sigma^{2}k(n-k)^{3/2}}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{8\sqrt{3}}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}2(n-k)^{1/2}\frac{x}{(1-x^{2})^{1/2}}\Big[e^{\frac{(p-1)\tilde{S}_{k}}{2}\frac{x^{2}}{(n-k)(1-x^{2})}}-1\Big]dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{R^{2}n}{\sigma^{2}k(n-k)}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{8\sqrt{3}}\sum_{m\geq 1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{m!}\frac{(p-1)^{m}}{2^{m}}S_{k,\kappa}^{m}\kappa^{m}\frac{x^{2m+1}}{(1-x^{2})^{m+1/2}}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{R^{2}n}{\sigma^{2}k(n-k)}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{8\sqrt{3}}S_{k,\kappa}\sum_{m\geq 1}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}\frac{1}{m!}\frac{(p-1)^{m}}{2^{m}}\kappa^{m}\frac{x^{2m+1}}{(1-x^{2})^{3/2}}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{R^{2}n}{\sigma^{2}k(n-k)}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}\sqrt{p-1}}{8\sqrt{3}}\frac{R^{2}}{\kappa\sigma_{n,k}^{2}}\int_{0}^{\sqrt{1-S_{k,\kappa}}}\Big(e^{\frac{(p-1)\kappa x^{2}}{2}}-1\Big)\frac{x}{(1-x^{2})^{3/2}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{8\sqrt{3}}\frac{R^{4}\sigma_{n,k}^{-4}\sqrt{p-1}}{\kappa}\Big(e^{\frac{1}{2}(p-1)\kappa S_{k,\kappa}}-1\Big)\frac{\sigma_{n,k}\sqrt{\kappa}}{R}.

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{8\sqrt{3}}\frac{R^{3}\sigma_{n,k}^{-3}({p-1})}{\sqrt{C}}\Big(e^{\frac{1}{2}C}-1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{8\sqrt{3}}\frac{R^{5/2}\sigma_{n,k}^{-3}({p-1})}{\sqrt{2}}\Big(e^{R^{2}}-1\Big).

Hence, if
p\leq\sigma_{n,k}^{2}+1\leq\frac{n\sigma^{2}}{4}+1
, this implies that

\displaystyle\omega^{R}_{p}(n,k,\sigma)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Omega^{R}_{p}(n,k,\sigma)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hyperref@@ii[KRsig]{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{K_{R,\sigma}}}p+(p-1)\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma}\Big[1+\frac{\sqrt{p-1}}{\sqrt{n}}\Big]+\sqrt{p-1}^{3}\frac{1}{2\sigma_{n,k}\sqrt{1/2}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{p-1}R^{-\frac{4}{p}}2^{\frac{3}{p}}R^{4/p}\sigma^{2/p-1}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{3}\frac{\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}R(p-1)}{2}\Big(e^{R^{2}}-1\Big)\log\Big(\frac{1+\sqrt{1/2}}{1-\sqrt{1/2}}\Big)

\displaystyle+R^{-\frac{4}{p}}\bigg[\sqrt{p-1}\tilde{R}^{2/p}4^{1/p}\sigma^{-1}\sigma_{n,k}^{-1}+{2}\bigg]\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{8\sqrt{3}}\frac{R^{5/2}({p-1})}{\sqrt{2}}\Big(e^{R^{2}}-1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle p\Big\{\hyperref@@ii[KRsig]{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{K_{R,\sigma}}}+\frac{R^{2}}{\sigma}(1+\sigma/2)+2^{\frac{3}{p}}\sigma^{2/p-1}\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{3}\frac{R^{2}}{2}\Big(e^{R^{2}}-1\Big)\log\Big(\frac{1+\sqrt{1/2}}{1-\sqrt{1/2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+R^{-\frac{4}{p}}\bigg[\tilde{R}^{2/p}4^{1/p}\sigma^{-1}+{2}\bigg]\frac{e^{19/300}\pi^{1/4}}{8\sqrt{3}}\frac{R^{5/2}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big(e^{R^{2}}-1\Big)\Big\}.

Now in the second case where
p>\sigma_{n,k}^{2}+1
, we have

\displaystyle\omega_{p}^{R}(n,k,\sigma)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{n,k}\sqrt{p-1}(1+\sqrt{2}R\sigma^{-1})

\displaystyle\leq p(1+\sqrt{2}R\sigma^{-1}).

Hence the desired result directly follows.

∎

Appendix D
Proof of results in
section

2.3

Proof of
theorem

2.2
.

The proof proceeds by induction. First, if
L=0
, then we remark that
n=1
and
W_{k}=0
. Therefore, if we choose
\tilde{Z}_{k}=0
, we know that it satisfies
1
with
\mathcal{I}=\llbracket 0,2^{L}\rrbracket
and is such that

\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq 2^{L}\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |W_{k}-\tilde{Z}_{k}|>\delta^{L}_{k}\Big)=0=\beta_{0}.

If
L\geq 1
, suppose there exists a Gaussian vector
(\tilde{Z}^{1}_{k})_{k\leq 2^{L-1}}
satisfying
1
with
\mathcal{I}=\llbracket 0,2^{L-1}\rrbracket
, and such that

\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq 2^{L-1}\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |W_{k}-\tilde{Z}_{k}|>\delta^{L-1}_{k}\Big)\leq\beta_{L-1}.

Then according to
lemma

2.1
there exists
(\tilde{Z}_{k})_{k\leq 2^{L}}
satisfying
1
with
\mathcal{I}=\llbracket 1,2^{L}\rrbracket
such that

\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq 2^{L-1}\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |W_{k}-\tilde{Z}_{k}|>\delta^{L}_{k}\Big)\leq 2\beta_{L-1}-\beta_{L-1}^{2}+\alpha_{0}.

Now, by definition,
2\beta_{L-1}-\beta_{L-1}^{2}+\alpha_{0}=\beta_{L},
which concludes the proof.∎

Proof of
corollary

2.2.1
.

First, according to
lemma

B.3
, we know that there exists
Z_{n}\in\sigma(S_{n},U_{n})
such that

Z_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2}n)

and, for
p\geq 2
, we have

\mathbb{P}\Big(|S_{n}-Z_{n}|\geq\frac{\mathcal{W}_{p}(S_{n},\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2}n))}{\alpha_{1}^{1/p}}\Big)\leq\alpha_{1}.

Now, by
lemma

K.9
, we know that for all
p\geq 2
, we have

\mathcal{W}_{p}(S_{n},\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2}n))\leq s_{p}^{R}(n,\sigma).

Hence, this directly implies that

\mathbb{P}\Big(|S_{n}-Z_{n}|\geq\delta^{*}(\alpha_{1})\Big)\leq\alpha_{1}.

Now according to
theorem

2.2

there exists a Gaussian vector
(\tilde{Z}_{k})_{k\leq n}
satisfying
1
with
\mathcal{I}=\llbracket 1,n\rrbracket
such that

\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |W_{k}-\tilde{Z}_{k}|\geq\delta_{k}\Big)\leq\beta_{L}.

Now note that
1
implies
(\tilde{Z}_{k})|Z_{n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\tilde{\Sigma})
where
\tilde{\Sigma}_{i,j}=\sigma^{2}\frac{i\wedge j(n-j\vee i)}{n}
. Hence, using the conjugacy of Gaussian priors with Gaussian likelihoods, we obtain

\Big(\tilde{Z}_{k}+\frac{k}{n}Z_{n}\Big)_{k\leq n}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\Sigma_{n}).

Hence if we define

Z_{k}:=\tilde{Z}_{k}+\frac{k}{n}Z_{n},

then by a union bound argument we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ |Z_{k}-S_{k}|\leq\delta_{k}+\frac{k}{n}\delta_{1}^{*}(\alpha_{1})\Big)\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(|S_{n}-Z_{n}|\leq\delta_{1}^{*}(\alpha_{1})\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ |\tilde{Z}_{k}-W_{k}|\leq\delta_{k}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\beta_{L}+\alpha_{1}.

∎

Proof of
theorem

2.3
.

By the definition of
\nu^{*}_{0}
, we know that
\beta_{L}(\nu_{0}^{*})\leq\alpha.
Hence, according to
theorem

2.2
, there exists
\tilde{Z}:=(\tilde{Z}_{k})_{0\leq k\leq n}
with the same covariance as
(W_{k})
, such that the following inequality holds:

\mathbb{P}(\exists k\leq n\textrm{ s.t. }\big|W_{k}-\tilde{Z}_{k}\big|\geq\Delta_{k}(\alpha))\leq\beta_{L}(\nu_{0}^{*})\leq\alpha.

∎

Proof of
theorem

2.4
.

A direct consequence of
theorem

2.3
combined with
corollary

2.2.1
.

∎

Appendix E
Proof of
proposition

3.1

Proof.

Define
\tilde{W}_{k}:=\sigma^{-1}W_{k}
and
\tilde{S}_{k}:=\sigma^{-1}S_{k}.
Using
theorems

2.4
and
2.3
we know that there exists centered Gaussian vectors
(\tilde{Z}^{*}_{k}),(Z^{*}_{k})
that have the same variance as respectively
(\tilde{W}_{k})
and
(\tilde{S}_{k})
such that

\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\tilde{S}_{k}-Z^{*}_{k}|\geq\mathcal{D}_{k}(\alpha(1-\rho),\frac{R}{\sigma},1)\Big)\leq\alpha(1-\rho),

and

\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\tilde{W}_{k}-\tilde{Z}^{*}_{k}|\geq\Delta_{k}(\alpha(1-\rho),\frac{R}{\sigma},1)\Big)\leq\alpha(1-\rho).

Define
Z_{k}:=\sigma Z_{k}^{*}
and
\tilde{Z}_{k}:=\sigma\tilde{Z}_{k}^{*}
. We remark that
(Z_{k})
and
(\tilde{Z}_{k})
are centered Gaussian vectors that have the same variance as
(S_{k})
and
(W_{k}).
Moreover, we note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |S_{k}-Z_{k}|\geq\sigma\mathcal{D}_{k}(\alpha(1-\rho),\frac{R}{\sigma},1)\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\tilde{S}_{k}-Z^{*}_{k}|\geq\mathcal{D}_{k}(\alpha(1-\rho),\frac{R}{\sigma},1)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha(1-\rho).

Similarly, we have that

\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\tilde{W}_{k}-\tilde{Z}^{*}_{k}|\geq\Delta_{k}(\alpha(1-\rho),\frac{R}{\sigma},1)\Big)\leq\alpha(1-\rho).

Moreover, according to
lemma

K.11
, we know that
R\mapsto\Delta_{k}(\alpha(1-\rho),R,1)
is an increasing function of
R
. Hence the following holds

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |S_{k}-Z_{k}|\geq\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{U}\penalty\ \mathcal{D}_{k}(\alpha(1-\rho),\tilde{R}_{k},1)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ \sigma\not\in[\sigma_{k}^{L},\sigma_{k}^{U}]\Big)+\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |S_{k}-Z_{k}|\geq\sigma\penalty\ \mathcal{D}_{k}(\alpha(1-\rho),R/\sigma,1)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho\alpha+(1-\rho)\alpha=\alpha.

Similarly, we can prove that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\leq n\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |W_{k}-\tilde{Z}_{k}|\geq\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{U}\penalty\ \Delta_{k}(\alpha(1-\rho),\tilde{R}_{k},1)\Big)\leq\alpha.

∎

Appendix F
Proof of
proposition

4.1

Proof.

Let
\delta_{1},\delta_{2}\in(0,1)
be such that
\delta>\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}
and set
\beta>1
. We denote
\delta_{3}:=\delta-\delta_{1}-\delta_{2},
and define
\delta_{2,i}:=\frac{\delta_{2}\beta^{-i}}{\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\beta^{-j}}.

The main idea of the proof is to approximate the statistics
\mathcal{T}_{s,t}
by the same statistics defined on Gaussians instead. The latter is then bounded using
[
31
]
. More precisely, by a union bound argument, we remark that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists s<t\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\mathcal{T}_{s,t}|\geq\mathcal{C}_{s,t}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists s<t\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\mathcal{T}_{s,t}|\geq\mathcal{C}_{s,t}\text{ and }\forall k,\penalty\ \sigma\in[\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{L},\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{U}]\Big)+\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists k\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ \sigma\not\in[\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{L},\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{U}]\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{1}+\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists s<t\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\mathcal{T}_{s,t}|\geq\mathcal{C}_{s,t}\text{ and }\forall k,\penalty\ \sigma\in[\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{L},\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{U}]\Big).

To further bound this, we first remark that

\mathcal{T}_{s,t}=\frac{t}{s(t-s)}\big(S_{s}-\frac{s}{t}S_{t}),

and hence resembles the quantities controlled in
algorithm

2
. A difficulty, however, is that
algorithm

2
controls the variation of
S_{k}-\frac{k}{n}S_{n}
uniformly over
k
, but only for a fixed sample size
n
. In this setting, we instead need to control
\mathcal{T}_{s,t}
uniformly over both
s
and
t
. To overcome this limitation, we first control
(\mathcal{T}_{s,t})_{s<t}
for different
t
that are expressible using powers of
2
, and then extend this control to all
t
.

In this goal, we define
\mathcal{I}^{-}_{k}=\sum_{i\leq\ell_{\mathbf{L}}(k)}2^{L_{i}}
and
\mathcal{I}^{+}_{k}=\sum_{i\leq u_{\mathbf{L}}(k)}2^{L_{i}}
, and remark that
\mathcal{I}^{-}_{k}\leq k\leq\mathcal{I}^{+}_{k}
.
Moreover, we define the increments

S^{*}_{\mathcal{I}^{+}_{k}}:=S_{\mathcal{I}^{+}_{k}}-S_{\mathcal{I}^{-}_{k}},\qquad\tilde{W}_{k}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(k)}:=S_{k}-S_{\mathcal{I}^{-}_{k}}-\frac{k-\mathcal{I}^{-}_{k}}{\mathcal{I}^{+}_{k}-\mathcal{I}^{-}_{k}}S^{*}_{\mathcal{I}^{+}_{k}}.

For all
i\in\mathbb{N}
, we write
N_{i}=\sum_{j\leq i}2^{L_{j}}
and let
(\tilde{\Delta}_{k}(\delta_{2,i}))_{u_{\mathbf{L}}(k)=i}
and
\tilde{\delta}^{*}_{N_{i}}(\delta_{2,i})
the output of
algorithm

4
with confidence
\delta_{2,i}
,
n_{1}=N_{i-1}
, and
n_{2}=N_{i}
. Note that by definition,
I_{k}^{+}=N_{u_{\mathbf{L}}(k)}
and
I_{k}^{-}=N_{\ell_{\mathbf{L}}(k)}
. For the ease of notation, we shorthand
\tilde{\delta}_{i}^{*}:=\tilde{\delta}^{*}_{N_{i}}(\delta_{2,i})
and
\tilde{\Delta}_{k}:=\tilde{\Delta}_{k}(\delta_{2,i}).

We remark that, for all
i\in\mathbb{N}
, according to
lemma

K.12
there exist Gaussian vectors
(\tilde{Z}^{i}_{k})
and
(Z^{*}_{i})
that have the same mean and variance as
(\tilde{W}^{i}_{k})
and
(S^{*}_{i})
such that

\mathbb{P}\Big(\max_{k,\penalty\ {u^{\mathbf{L}}}(k)=i}\penalty\ (\tilde{\Delta}_{k})^{-1}\big|\tilde{W}_{k}^{i}-\tilde{Z}_{k}^{i}\big|\leq 1,\quad|S^{*}_{i}-Z^{*}_{i}|\leq\tilde{\delta}_{N_{i}}^{*},\penalty\ \sigma\in\bigcap_{k}[\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{L},\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{U}]\Big)\geq 1-\delta_{2,i}.

(32)

For each
k
we define

Z_{k}:=\sum_{j\leq\ell_{\mathbf{L}(k)}}Z^{*}_{j}+\tilde{Z}_{k}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(k)}+\frac{s}{\mathcal{I}_{k}^{+}-\mathcal{I}_{k}^{-}}Z^{*}_{\mathcal{I}_{k}^{+}}.

We note that
(Z_{k})
is a Gaussian process that has the same mean and variance as
(S_{k}).

Interestingly, both
\mathcal{T}_{s,t}
and
\mathcal{T}_{s,t}(Z):=\frac{1}{s}Z_{s}-\frac{1}{t-s}(Z_{t}-Z_{s})
can be re-expressed in terms of
(\tilde{W}_{k}^{i})
,
(S^{*}_{i})
,
(\tilde{Z}_{k}^{i})
, and
(Z^{*}_{i})
. Indeed, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{s(t-s)}{t}\mathcal{T}_{s,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle S_{s}-\frac{s}{t}S_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{W}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(s)}_{s}-\frac{s}{t}\tilde{W}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(t)}_{t}+\sum_{k\leq\ell_{\mathbf{L}}(s)}S_{k}^{*}\big(1-\frac{s}{t}\big)+\Big(\frac{s-I_{s}^{-}}{I_{s}^{+}-I_{s}^{-}}-\frac{s}{t}\Big)S^{*}_{u_{\mathbf{L}(s)}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{s}{t}\sum_{u_{\mathbf{L}}(s)+1\leq k\leq\ell_{\mathbf{L}}(t)}S_{k}^{*}-\frac{s}{t}\frac{t-I_{t}^{-}}{I_{t}^{+}-I_{t}^{-}}S^{*}_{u_{\mathbf{L}}(t)},

and

\displaystyle\frac{s(t-s)}{t}\mathcal{T}_{s,t}(Z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{Z}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(s)}_{s}-\frac{s}{t}\tilde{Z}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(t)}_{t}+\sum_{k\leq\ell_{\mathbf{L}}(s)}Z_{k}^{*}\big(1-\frac{s}{t}\big)+\Big(\frac{s-I_{s}^{-}}{I_{s}^{+}-I_{s}^{-}}-\frac{s}{t}\Big)Z^{*}_{u_{\mathbf{L}(s)}}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{s}{t}\sum_{u_{\mathbf{L}}(s)+1\leq k\leq\ell_{\mathbf{L}}(t)}Z_{k}^{*}-\frac{s}{t}\frac{t-I_{t}^{-}}{I_{t}^{+}-I_{t}^{-}}Z^{*}_{u_{\mathbf{L}}(t)}.

Using
eq.

32
combined with a union bound, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists s\leq t\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\Big|\mathcal{T}_{s,t}-\mathcal{T}_{s,t}(Z)\Big|\geq g_{\beta}(t,s,\delta_{2})\frac{t}{s(t-s)},\penalty\ \sigma\in\bigcap_{k}[\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{L},\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{U}]\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{2},

where
g_{\beta}(t,s,\delta_{2})
is defined as follows: if
u_{\mathbf{L}}(s)\neq u_{\mathbf{L}}(t)
, then

\displaystyle g_{\beta}(t,s,\delta_{2})=

\displaystyle\sum_{k\leq\ell_{\mathbf{L}}(s)}\tilde{\delta}_{k}^{*}\big(1-\frac{s}{t}\big)+\frac{s}{t}\sum_{u_{\mathbf{L}}(s)+1\leq k\leq\ell_{\mathbf{L}}(t)}\tilde{\delta}_{k}^{*}+\frac{s}{t}\frac{t-I_{t}^{-}}{I_{t}^{+}-I_{t}^{-}}\tilde{\delta}^{*}_{u_{\mathbf{L}}(t)}

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{s-I_{s}^{-}}{I_{s}^{+}-I_{s}^{-}}-\frac{s}{t}\Big)\tilde{\delta}^{*}_{u_{\mathbf{L}(s)}}+\tilde{\Delta}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(s)}_{s}+\frac{s}{t}\tilde{\Delta}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(t)}_{t},

and if
u_{\mathbf{L}}(s)=u_{\mathbf{L}}(t)
, then

\displaystyle g_{\beta}(t,s,\delta_{2})=

\displaystyle\sum_{k\leq\ell_{\mathbf{L}}(s)}\tilde{\delta}_{k}^{*}\big(1-\frac{s}{t}\big)+\frac{s}{t}\tilde{\delta}^{*}_{N_{u_{\mathbf{L}(s)}}}+\tilde{\Delta}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(s)}_{s}+\frac{s}{t}\tilde{\Delta}^{u_{\mathbf{L}}(t)}_{t}.

Moreover according to Theorem 5 of
[
31
]
we know that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists s\leq t\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\mathcal{T}_{s,t}(Z)|\geq\hat{\sigma}_{t}^{U}\sqrt{\frac{t}{s(t-s)}}\sqrt{(1+\frac{1}{t})2\log(\frac{2(t-1)\sqrt{t+1})}{\delta_{3}})},\sigma\in\bigcap_{k}[\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{L},\hat{\sigma}_{k}^{U}]\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\exists s\leq t\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\mathcal{T}_{s,t}(Z)|\geq\sigma\sqrt{\frac{t}{s(t-s)}}\sqrt{(1+\frac{1}{t})2\log(\frac{2(t-1)\sqrt{t+1}}{\delta_{3}})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta_{3},

where the last inequality is due to Theorem 5 of
[
31
]
.
Therefore, by a union bound argument, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{H_{0}}\Big(\exists s<t\penalty\ \textrm{s.t.}\penalty\ |\mathcal{T}_{s,t}|\geq\mathcal{C}_{s,t}\Big)\leq\delta.

∎

Appendix G
Proof of
proposition

4.3

Proof.

For each
N
, we define
Y^{N}_{i}:=X_{i}^{N}+\frac{R}{2}
and remark that
(Y^{N}_{i})
are almost surely positive

Y^{N}_{1}\overset{a.s}{\geq}0,
and are bounded by
R
meaning that
Y^{N}_{1}\overset{a.s}{\leq}R.
Moreover we write

\bar{W}_{i}^{N}:=W_{i}^{N}-i\mu_{N}=\sum_{j\leq i}Y_{j}^{N}-\mathbb{E}(Y_{j}^{N}).

Using
theorem

2.4
, we know that there exists a Gaussian vector
(Z_{i}^{N})
with same mean and variance as
(\bar{W}_{i}^{N})
such that for all
\alpha>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\forall{i\leq N}\big|\bar{W}_{i}^{N}-Z_{i}^{N}\big|\geq\mathcal{D}_{i}(\alpha)\Big)\leq\alpha.

Hence we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\tau_{N}\geq N)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}(\forall i\leq N\penalty\ W_{i}^{N}\leq g_{i}^{N})

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Big(\max_{i\leq N}\big|\bar{W}_{i}^{N}-Z_{i}^{N}\big|\geq\mathcal{D}_{i}(\alpha)\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{P}\Big(\forall i\leq N,\penalty\ Z_{i}^{N}+i\mu_{n}\leq g_{i}^{N}+\mathcal{D}_{i}(\alpha)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha+\mathbb{P}\Big(\forall i\leq N\penalty\ Z_{i}^{N}+i\mu_{N}\leq g_{i}^{N}+\mathcal{D}_{i}(\alpha)\Big).

Now remark that
(\sigma_{N}^{-1}Z_{i}^{N})\overset{d}{=}(B_{t})_{t\in\mathbb{N},t\leq N}
. Hence we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\tau_{N}\geq N)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}(\forall i\leq N\penalty\ W_{i}^{N}\geq g_{i}^{N})

\displaystyle\leq\alpha+\mathbb{P}\Big(\forall i\leq N,\penalty\ B_{i}\leq\sigma_{N}^{-1}\{g_{i}^{N}-i\mu_{N}+\mathcal{D}_{i}(\alpha)\}\Big).

This establishes the first claim of
proposition

4.3
. The second claim is a direct consequence of
lemma

2.6
which implies that there exists a constant
\kappa^{R}<\infty
, that does not depend on
N
, such that

\displaystyle\max_{i\leq N}\big|\mathcal{D}_{i}(\alpha)\big|\leq\log(N)\kappa_{R}(\log(N)-\log(\alpha)).

∎

Proposition H.1
.

with covariance

and

such that the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\exists k\leq n\textrm{ s.t. }\big|S_{k}-{Z}_{k}\big|\geq\mathcal{D}_{k}(\alpha))\leq\alpha,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\exists k\leq n\textrm{ s.t. }\big|W_{k}-\tilde{Z}_{k}\big|\geq\Delta_{k}(\alpha))\leq\alpha.
